					value = conservative
					factor = 0.6
				}

					value = reactionary
					factor = 0.4
				}

					value = reactionary

				}
			}

				name = sakoku

			}
		}
	}
	japan_rangoku = {

		}
		allow = {
		}
		effect = {

						name = visionary_thinker
						duration = -1
					}
				}
			}
			random_owned = {
				limit = {
					owner = { NOT = { tag = SAT } }
				}
				owner = {		
					add_country_modifier = {
						name = military_man
						duration = -1
					}
				}
			}
			any_pop = {
				ideology = {
					value = conservative
					factor = 0.5
				}
				ideology = {
					value = reactionary
					factor = 0.1
				}
			}
			add_country_modifier = {
				name = sayings_of_the_dutch
				duration = -1
			}
			random_owned = {
				limit = { province_id = 1661 }
				any_pop = {
					ideology = {
						value = liberal
						factor = 0.2
					}
				}

					name = foreign_trading_post
					duration = -1
				}
			}
		}
	}

	}
